  **Codex lessons this session (in lessons-learned):** (a) create-list success audit MUST be written INSIDE the create transaction (InsertLogCoreAsync given the tx) — post-commit audit = partial-success failure mode; (b) every refused export/create attempt (gate/validation/cap/conflict/empty) writes a status=failed export_logs row via FailAsync — "returns an error code" ≠ "not silent"; (c) non-egress audit actions need honest enum values (format='list', delivery_mode='internal'), not faked csv/server_stream; (d) routing changes are auto-MEDIUM and MEDIUM verification questions must span Data+Auth categories.

**4 yeni lesson (arch/lessons-learned.md):**
1. Cross-subdomain localStorage isolation — voice.invekto.com ve app.invekto.com farklı origin, localStorage paylaşımı YOK. Dashboard JWT'yi cross-origin taşımak için URL-bridge (?token=) veya SameSite=None cookie tek portable kanal. localStorage same-origin assumption code-review'de yakalanmadıysa Codex CQ8/CQ9'da blocker.
2. JavaScript Number.parseInt prefix-based parsing bug — "12abc" → 12, "1e3" → 1, "0x10" → 0. localStorage / URL param / form input integer validation için strict regex /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ + Number.isSafeInteger gerekli (parsePositiveInt pattern).
3. cleanup() helper'ları user-visible status state'i TOUCH ETMEMELI — error label'ı cleanup zincirinde override edilir. Status reset = caller sorumluluğu (resetIdleStatus helper pattern). Error paths: setStatus AFTER cleanup() ile error label survive.
4. Browser-side INV diagnostic codes (INV-VR-CLIENT-001..010) — server INV codes ile disjoint range, display-only diagnostic surface. Tüm browser error path'leri INV code'la etiketli olmazsa Codex CQ12 blocker. errors.md'ye client-side codes entry'leri server logic dependency yaratmadan documentation purpose için eklenir.

**4 yeni lesson (arch/lessons-learned.md):**
1. Codex broad-catch strict interpretation: `catch (Exception ex) when (filter)` HALA broad sayılır. Explicit per-type catches gerekli (JsonException + InvalidOperationException + ArgumentException ayrı)
2. CancellationTokenRegistration IDisposable hygiene: sessionCt.Register dönen handle explicit dispose gerek; class IDisposable yap + finally Dispose
3. HUD/UI truth-correlation Codex expectation: local enqueue ≠ "delivered"; async messaging mimaride ACK-gated UI F4 backlog (Q FORCE PASS precedent gerek)
4. TryGetInt32 vs GetInt32 for JSON int parsing: GetInt32 FormatException + OverflowException atar, TryGetInt32(out bool) safer

**Bootstrap kararsızlık (~10 dk debug):**

3. **Fix:** bash background `export ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development + export OPENAI_API_KEY` + `dotnet run` (--no-build kaldırıldı), 4sn boot

Üçüncü attempt: yine İspanyolca, neden: `Unknown parameter: 'session.temperature'`.

**GA migration (incremental shape discovery — 5 iter):**
- **Iter 1:** `OpenAI-Beta: realtime=v1` header REMOVED (RealtimeApiClient.cs:67 line removed); GA Authorization Bearer only
- **Iter 2:** Model `gpt-4o-realtime-preview` → `gpt-realtime` (GA production model, hem appsettings.json hem appsettings.Development.json hem RealtimeSession.cs default)
- **Iter 3:** Event names update (RealtimeApiClient DispatchEvent switch): `response.audio.delta` → `response.output_audio.delta`, `response.audio_transcript.delta` → `response.output_audio_transcript.delta` (legacy fallback case'ler de eklendi)
- **Iter 4:** SessionConfig record `type: "realtime"` field eklendi (GA zorunlu, `session.type` ya `realtime` ya `transcription`)
- **Iter 5:** `modalities` REMOVED (GA otomatik audio+text emit), `voice`/`input_audio_format`/`output_audio_format`/`input_audio_transcription`/`turn_detection` tümü `session.audio.{input,output}` NESTED altına taşındı (yeni records: `SessionAudioConfig` + `SessionAudioInputConfig` + `SessionAudioOutputConfig`)
- **Iter 6:** `temperature` + `max_response_output_tokens` REMOVED session level (GA'da per-response `response.create` event'inde verilir, F0 default behavior yeter)

### Deploy

- **Deploy:** MCP `server-deploy` tool (SSH/SFTP, atomik: stop -> zip -> upload -> extract -> config restore -> start -> health)
- **Sunucu:** services.invekto.com, `C:\Invekto\{Service}\current\`
- **Service Manager:** NSSM (`C:\Invekto\nssm.exe`)
- **.NET Runtime:** ASP.NET Core 8.0.23

### Tech Stack

| Component | Technology |
|-----------|------------|
| Backend | .NET 8 Minimal API |
| Microservice | .NET 8 Minimal API + Windows Service |
| Shared | .NET 8 Class Library |
| Frontend | React 18 + TypeScript + Vite |
| DB | PostgreSQL 16 + pgvector |
| Logging | JSON Lines (custom) |

### Project Structure

```
src/
├── Invekto.Shared/           # DTOs, constants, logging, auth
├── Invekto.Backend/          # Port 5000 - API + Dashboard + FlowBuilder SPA
├── Invekto.ChatAnalysis/     # Port 7101 - Claude Haiku chat analysis
├── Invekto.Appointments/     # Port 7102 - Randevu + TreatmentLifecycle
├── Invekto.Knowledge/        # Port 7104 - RAG + pgvector + PDF
├── Invekto.AgentAI/          # Port 7105 - AI reply suggestion
├── Invekto.Integrations/     # Port 7106 - HB, kargo, external APIs
├── Invekto.Outbound/         # Port 7107 - Broadcast + campaigns + triggers
├── Invekto.Automation/       # Port 7108 - Chatbot + FlowEngine v2
├── Invekto.WhatsAppAnalytics/ # Port 7109 - NLP pipeline C# port
└── Invekto.Marketing/        # Port 7112 - Reviews, referrals, tourism
```
